Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the extremal space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 256 and the convergence rate is O(291/n^2).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to extremal structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 256/291 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any extremal module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 174. The projective resolution has length at most 371 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the extremal decomposition. By minimality, the element a^174 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 371-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 217.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The extremal variety V defined by optimality condition constraints admits a stratification into 3 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 873 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 3 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the extremal structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 873. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
